so `187.8 ms`.


## 2026-11-26 (muon sau): CE loss device + clean LN + SGD fix

- TokenVector.CudaBackend@18adb03, compile sach, max ldloc.s/stloc.s = 255/255.
- loss_kernel_packed (3 params, packed T/V/invT/ids + combined lossRow+dL) verified tren Kaggle T4:
  MAXERR 7.450581e-09, LAUNCH ok, LOSSROW0 1.4658537 / LOSSROW1 0.20593393, KET LUAN OK.
- Cau truc trainN: device gelu + packed CE device + LN backward sach (khong rename acc/dvv/gvv) +
  cap nhat trong so SGD dung tung tensor (trc do wph bi ghi de 6 lan -> toan bo gradient vao HW2).
- Can chay lai TRAIN_N de xac nhan LOSS 2 step (ky vong step 2 giam) + MS_STEP + gradient.
- Speed baseline van 187.8 ms; chua do lai.


## Ket thuc phien: CE device trong trainN chua xong

- packed loss kernel loss_kernel_packed da verify rieng: MAXERR 7.450581e-09 (Kaggle T4, HEAD 127a3ed).
- Tich hop vao 	rainN (clean LN c8df82f + device gelu + SGD fix) cho ket qua NaN:
  packed params dung (512, 50257, 0.001953125), module/FN rc=0, nhung logits nan -> LOSS nan ->
  backward nan -> step 2 cublas status 13. Da co lap bang marker: NaN xuat hien truoc CE,
  khong phai loi cua kernel loss (kernel da verify standalone).
- Da khoi phuc ban chay duoc cuoi cung (c7de056): LOSS 10.824877, 24 lop, MS_STEP 6828.25 ms.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@4910461 (restore), Inference@374d7c6.
- Chua dat 187.8 ms; fp16 tensor-core / AdamW chua lam.


## Cuoc dieu tra gradient NaN (ket thuc phien)

- wph bi ghi de 6 lan (tat ca gradient vao HW2) da sua: update dung HWq/HWk/HWv/HWo/HW1/HW2.
- exp(2*z) khong co clamp trong vong gelu recompute cua backward da them clamp.
- Tuy nhien van NaN: step1 forward finite (LOSS 10.8249), gHH0=1.9e-6, gOUT0=8.5e-9 finite,
  nhung dW1[0]/dW2[0] = nan o moi lop; lr=1e-3 lam LOSS step2 = nan.
- Ket luan: loi nam o duong weight-gradient GEMM/update (co the device-buffer aliasing hoac
  cuBLAS status sticky do D2D copy qua wrapper cuda_h2d), KHONG phai CE/softmax/gelu.
- Chua co fix da verify. Da khoi phuc ban chay duoc cuoi (c7de056) tren HEAD 80da502.
- CE device rieng van da verify MAXERR 7.45e-09.


## Dung trace loop / trang thai cuoi

- Da thay the D2D copy (kind 1 -> 3) tai 3 vi tri; van NaN o step 2.
- Dump truc tiep tai layer dau: GZ 1.9e-6, DY2 -1.66, DH 17.36, G1D 0.0, G2D 8.0e-5 (deu finite).
  NaN xuat hien o mot layer sau do trong backward (24 lop dung chung device buffer), khong phai GEMM dau tien.
- Da dung viec them marker + chay lai Kaggle (~20 lan) vi khong hieu qua; khoi phuc ban verify duoc cuoi.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@7986afc.
- Huong tiep theo (can viec lon, khong phai patch nho): viet lai script trainN moi dung cau truc backward da verify cua
  ilgpt2_bwd_clean.tkv (16 gradient <=1.1e-5), buffer rieng tung lop, roi moi them CE device.


## Rewrite sach (TokenVector-only)

- ilgpt2_trainN_clean.tkv: viet lai tu 09755b1, moi lop backward tu nap activation per-layer
  (y1d/y2d/pd/zd) + tinh lai dK/dV/dA/dHH, D2D kind=3, SGD tung tensor, clamp gelu,
  NaN/inf guard khi update. Compile sach max local 254.
- Kaggle: loss step1/step2 finite 10.82487678527832 voi lr=1e-7/1e-5, delta 0;
  lr=1e-4/1e-3 lam loss step2 nan (nan da co trong buffer weight truoc update).
- Khong claim hoc duoc o scale 24 lop; gate chi yeu cau finite loss + marks.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@c873081.


## Boc tach NaN: ket luan cuoi

- So sanh tung phep tinh voi ilgpt2_bwd_clean.tkv: da bo sung recompute dQ/dK/dV/dA/dHH
  dau moi backward layer, D2D kind=3, clamp gelu, SGD tung tensor.
- Thu nghiem lr=0: LOSS step2 finite -> SGD update la nguon dua NaN vao weight (khong phai GEMM/CE/softmax).
- Da thu NaN guard (dxv != dxv) va guard khoang bang if/else: van nan -> co che guard khong hoat dong
  trong TokenVector (so sanh f32 voi literal lon).
- Chua tim ra cach triet tieu tai goc; khong claim hoc duoc o scale 24 lop.
- HEAD: TokenVector.CudaBackend@aeb5fe0 (rewrite on dinh, lr=1e-7).

## Tim ra nguyen nhan goc (NaN + loss khong giam)

Bang chung: `LOSS 10.824877` = dung `ln(50257)` -> logits dong nhat tren moi `v`.

1. **Harness degenerate**: trong `hWh` (head weight) moi phan tu = 0.20 nen
   `logit_v = 0.2 * sum_h x_h` giong het nhau voi moi `v` -> softmax deu ->
   loss = `ln(V)` **khong the giam**, du update co dung.
   => `delta = 0` khong phai loi update.
2. **Khoi tao qua lon**: weight ~0.2 voi `H=1024` cho gain `0.2*sqrt(1024)=6.4`
   moi matmul; 24 lop => `6.4^24 ~ 1e19` -> gradient tran -> NaN.
   => `lr>=1e-4` NaN khong phai loi guard.
3. **Thieu gradient residual** trong backward: `OUT = X2 + O2`, `X2 = hX + hO`
   nhung `dL/dX2` va `dL/dhX` khong cong `gOUT`; cho bu lai dung nham `AO` (=OUT)
   thay vi `gOUT`.

### Sua
- init weight scale ~0.03 (gain ~0.96/lop), head weight phu thuoc `v` -> pha vo doi xung.
- input `hX` them thanh phan phu thuoc vi tri.
- backward: nap `gOUT` vao `hOUT` dau moi lop, cong residual o ca LN2 va LN1.
- `lr = 0.02`; SGD kernel PTX scrub NaN bang `max/min/add` (khong predicate).
- Gop module load ra khoi vong update (`sgd_init` goi 1 lan).
- Giai phong 19 local (bo HWq..HW2, HGq..HG2, gWqh..gW2h khong con dung) -> max local 251.

HEAD: `TokenVector.CudaBackend@394f4e6`. Cho ket qua do tren Kaggle.
## Nguyen nhan that cua inf/NaN: doc bo nho heap chua khoi tao

`alloc_bytes` -> `Marshal.AllocHGlobal` = bo nho **chua khoi tao**.
`hP` (ma tran softmax) chi duoc ghi **nua tam giac duoi** (`jj <= ii`);
nua tren khong bao gio duoc ghi -> **rac heap**, nhung van duoc copy vao `dP`
va nhan voi `V`: `A = P * V`.

He qua: `A` nhan mot luong nhieu ngau nhien lon tu nua tren, nen
`OUTMAX` nhay loan giua cac lan chay (867 / 111 / 9.6e8) va NaN xuat hien
khong tat dinh. Backward cung doc `hP` cho `dS = P*(gP - rowsum)` nen
gradient cung bi rac.

### Sua
- `zero_buf(hP, T*T)` goi **mot lan** truoc vong train; nua tren giu 0 vinh vien
  (vi softmax chi ghi lai nua duoi).
- Bo 4 buffer host chet `hyq/hyk/hyv/hy1` (8 MB) -> max local **254 -> 185**.
- Them `scan(tag, buf, n)` in `M SCAN <tag> <maxabs> <nan_count>`:
  tag 1 = OUT, tag 2 = logits, tag 3 = `gWq` lop 0 (do gradient that).
- `lr = 1e-4` (truoc do 0.01 qua lon: `sum g^2 ~ 1e4` nen buoc dau tien
  da nhay qua cuc tieu).

HEAD: `TokenVector.CudaBackend@4fffb36`. Cho ket qua Kaggle.
## Lan 20: hP da sach nhung van inf -> hai nguyen nhan moi

Ket qua: `M SCAN 1 111.12657 0` / `M SCAN 2 72.47433 0` / `LOSS inf` /
`M SCAN 3 inf 525966`.

1. **`inf` khong phai do NaN**: ca OUT lan logits deu co `nan_count = 0`.
   Nguyen nhan la **underflow cua `exp`**: dai logit ~145 nen
   `exp(lv - mx)` = 0 o f32 -> `pc = 0` -> `log(0) = -inf`.
   => Chuyen CE sang **log-space**: `loss -= (lt - mx - log(zs))`.

2. **`OUTMAX` = 111 khong doi** du `hP` da duoc zero => `P` khong phai
   nguon khuech dai. Thu pham la `wrand`: no dung
   `((i%251)*(j%251))%251` nen co **chu ky 251 o ca hai chieu**, va `hX`
   cung tuan hoan 251 -> `sum_i Y1[i] W[i,j]` **cong huong** thay vi triet tieu
   (Q ~ 20 thay vi 0.37, S ~ 1e4, softmax gan one-hot).
   => Thay bang mixer kieu xxhash:
   `h = i*374761393 + j*668265263; h = (h ^ (h>>13))*1274126177; h ^= h>>16`.
   Kiem chung doc lap (PowerShell): mean 0.002, std 0.2885, histogram 8 bin
   phang, khong lap lai theo chu ky 251.

3. **Residual khong noi**: `hX` khong bao gio duoc gan bang `hOUT` nen ca 24
   lop doc cung mot input (comment ghi "hoac OUT lop truoc" nhung thieu code).
   => Them `copy_buf` va `hX = hOUT` cuoi moi lop; khoi phuc `hX = hIN`
   dau moi buoc de input khong bi bien doi qua cac buoc.

4. Bo mot luot gelu thua trong backward (T*F phep `exp` moi lop).

`scan()` gio in them **mean-square** de hieu chuan `lr` tu gradient do duoc.
`lr = 0.5` (uoc luong `sum g^2 ~ 0.1` cho `Delta loss ~ -0.05`).

HEAD: `TokenVector.CudaBackend@fd2bd61`. max local 186, exe 35328 bytes.

## Run 22 (2026-11-27 06:53 +0700) - HEAD `TokenVector.CudaBackend@4f2f22d`

Forward da sach (`M SCAN 10 1.0648886`, `M SCAN 2 5.0969634`, `LOSS 11.181591`,
`nan_count = 0`). Loi con lai: buoc 2 no ra `OUT 1.85e12` / `LOSS 5.03e11`.

### Loi chan doan da tim ra
`scan(3, hGDX2, H*H)` doc **4 MB** tu buffer `hGDX2` chi duoc cap **2 MB**
(`alloc_bytes(qb)`), va `cuda_h2d` chi copy `qb` byte. Nua sau cua lan scan la
bo nho khong duoc khoi tao => `3.32e38 / 1620 nan` la **artifact**, khong phai
gradient that. Da tach `hgW = alloc_bytes(whb)` (4 MB) va copy du `whb` byte.

### Them thang do gradient (tat ca dung kich thuoc buffer)
| tag | noi dung | kich thuoc |
|-----|----------|-----------|
| 4   | `gOUT` ngay sau head gemm (`dDL^T * Wh`) | `T*H` |
| 54  | `gHH` lop 23 | `T*F` |
| 55  | `gZ`  lop 23 | `T*F` |
| 50  | `dL/dX2` lop 23 | `T*H` |
| 51  | `gP` lop 23 | `T*T` |
| 52  | `gQ` lop 23 | `T*H` |
| 53  | `gWq` lop 23 | `H*H` |
| 40  | `dL/dhX` lop 23 | `T*H` |
| 41  | `dL/dhX` lop 12 | `T*H` |
| 42  | `dL/dhX` lop 0  | `T*H` |
| 3   | `gWq` lop 0 | `H*H` |

Uoc luong ly thuyet (gain 0.04, hal = 1/sqrt(H), loss chia T):
`gOUT ~ 2e-5`, `gP ~ 1e-4`, `gQ ~ 1e-7`, `gWq ~ 1e-6`, `dL/dhX ~ 1e-5`.
Neu mot tag lon hon ~1e6 lan => khuech dai o dung tang do.

max local 188 (<= 255), exe 35328 bytes.

### Run 22 - ket qua do (HEAD `4f2f22d`)

```
M SCAN 4  2.3572116e-05   0   1.6809872e-10   <- gOUT head  (dung)
M SCAN 54 2.438499e-05    0   2.2904633e-11   <- gHH lop 23 (dung)
M SCAN 55 2.2143755e-05   0   7.3942545e-12   <- gZ  lop 23 (dung)
M SCAN 50 21.463848       0   17.647236       <- dL/dX2 lop 23  == M SCAN 1 !
M SCAN 51 598.71045       0   56450.32
M SCAN 52 3.8030431       0   0.07621061
M SCAN 53 223.79373       0   672.1243
M SCAN 40 21.28449        0   17.685993
M SCAN 41 21.1565         0   17.938654
M SCAN 42 254.19131       0   599.72986
M SCAN 3  46.37529        0   89.80882
```

`M SCAN 50` trung **tung chu so** voi `M SCAN 1` (21.463848 / 17.647236)
=> `hGDX2` chua chinh OUT cua forward, khong phai gradient.

### Nguyen nhan goc (da fix, HEAD `a5dce8c`)

```tkv
wh = cuda_h2d(gOUT, hOUT, qb, 2)   # dst=device, src=host, kind=D2H  -> SAI
```

`cudaMemcpy` kiem tra huong con tro theo `kind`, tra `cudaErrorInvalidValue`
va **khong copy**. `wh` khong duoc kiem tra nen loi im lang; `hOUT` giu
nguyen OUT cua forward trong suot backward. Moi lop cong OUT (21 -> 254)
vao `dL/dhX` thay vi `dL/dOUT` (~2e-5), nen gradient lon hon that ~1e6 lan
va `gWq lop 0 = 46.4` thay vi ~1e-4. SGD `lr=0.5` -> `dW ~ 23` ->
buoc 2 `OUT 1.85e12`, `LOSS 5.03e11`.

Day la **copy kind-2 nguoc chieu duy nhat** trong file (55 lenh con lai deu
dung thu tu `host_dst, dev_src`); `ilgpt2_bwd_clean.tkv` cung dung thu tu
nen ket qua verify 16 gradient `<= 1.1e-5` van hieu luc.

Fix: `cuda_h2d(hOUT, gOUT, qb, 2)` + kiem tra `wh != 0`.

Uoc luong sau fix (gradient tuyen tinh theo `dX2`):
`dX2 ~ 2.4e-5`, `gP ~ 7e-4`, `gQ ~ 4e-6`, `gWq ~ 3e-5`,
`sum g^2 ~ 0.3` => voi `lr = 0.5` ky vong `loss delta ~ -0.1`.

## Run 24 - GELU len device (HEAD `f55ae4f`)

Thay 3 vong `exp` host moi lop bang 2 PTX kernel:
- `gelu_fwd_kernel(x, y, n)` - copy nguyen van tu probe da verify.
- `gelu_bwd_kernel(x, dy, dx, n)` - `dx = dy * (0.5(1+t) + 0.5x(1-t^2)*0.79788456*(1+0.134145x^2))`,
  `t = tanh.approx(0.79788456(x + 0.044715x^3))`.
- `gelu_selftest()` chay truoc vung do (n=1024, x in [-4,4)) va in
  `GELU SELFTEST MAXERR`; > 1e-4 thi dung.
- Bo toan bo scan trong vong lap (ke ca `scan 2` doc 25.7M phan tu logits)
  va mot `cuda_h2d(hY2, y2d, qb, 2)` chet (2 MB/lop).

Khong con host `exp` nao trong vong lap lop. max local 183, exe 61440 bytes.
Ky vong: loss gan nhu khong doi (11.181591 / ~10.8605), `MS_STEP` giam tu 7853.

## Run 26 - full fp16 tensor-core GEMMs (HEAD `0fff60c` / `c6f08ec`)

`cublasGemmEx(CUDA_R_16F in, CUDA_R_32F out, CUBLAS_COMPUTE_32F)`,
fp32 master weights + SGD in device PTX. Two pooled fp16 scratch buffers
`fpA`/`fpB` keep IL locals at 193 <= 255.

- `lr=0.5`: delta only `-0.0146` - SGD updates ~1.5e-6 are ~1/10 of fp16 ULP
  at weight magnitude ~0.01, so they round away when weights are cast to fp16.
- `lr=8.0`: `LOSS 11.181629 -> 10.890945`, delta `-0.290685`, `MS_STEP 270.07`, OK.

```
LOSS  11.181629 -> 10.891400
loss delta = -0.29022884368896484
MS_LAYERS 111.62   MS_HEAD 257.43   MS_BWD 88.15
MS_TOTAL 529.90    MS_STEP 264.95
KET LUAN: OK  (2 fwd / 2 bwd / 2 upd, 0 FAIL)
```

Timing breakdown (2 steps): head+loss 257 ms is the single biggest cost,
24 forward layers 112 ms, backward 88 ms, remainder ~73 ms (SGD updates).

### Speed status

| path | ms/step |
|------|---------|
| torch eager | 442.3 |
| C++-generated bridge (target) | **187.8** (`2.36x` vs torch) |
| IL-only fp32 device kernels | 453.7 |
| IL-only fp16 tensor core | **264.9** |

IL-only is now `1.67x` faster than torch eager but still `1.41x` slower than
the C++ bridge. Target NOT met.

### Failed speed experiments (reverted)

- Coalesced shared-memory loss kernel: PTX loaded but segfaulted .NET (rc -11).
- Coalesced single-thread-per-token loss with transposed head logits:
  CUDA_ERROR_ILLEGAL_ADDRESS (700) at layer-10 LN backward.
- `CUBLAS_COMPUTE_32F_FAST_16F` (74): no measurable change vs 68.
